<div><tool-call id="toolu_01VEV1HndJg4rj9uX9aNjcuC"><script type="application/json">{"version":2,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"functions/resolve_rm_site.R","content":"# resolve_rm_site.R\n#\n# Maps lab site labels such as \"RM 22\", \"RM22 DUP\", \"RM_10_DUP\", \"RM...[truncated, total 3214 chars]"},"caption":"functions/resolve_rm_site.R","captionLink":"functions/resolve_rm_site.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# resolve_rm_site.R\n#\n# Maps lab site labels such as \"RM 22\", \"RM22 DUP\", \"RM_10_DUP\", \"RM O\" to a\n# bare monitoring_location_id by parsing the river-mile number and matching it to\n# `river_mile` in reference/baseline_sites.csv (unique across the 22 sites).\n#\n# Sample condition is read from the label text, never from a list of sites, because\n# duplicate, field blank and trip blank locations change every year and season:\n#   \"DUP\"                      -> Field Duplicate\n#   \"BLANK\" with an RM number  -> Field Blank (or Trip Blank when \"TRIP\" is present)\n#   \"BLANK\" with no RM number  -> lab method blank, lab_qc_type \"MB\"\n#   \"POSITIVE\"                 -> lab positive control, lab_qc_type \"LCS\"\n#\n# Returns a tibble: label, monitoring_location_id (chr, no KENAI_WQX- prefix),\n# sample_condition, lab_qc_type. Stops, listing the labels, if a label that is not a\n# lab QC row cannot be matched to a site.\n#\n# Multi-site labels (containing \"&\", e.g. SGS \"RM1.5&6.5 trip blank\") are not\n# resolved here; they are expanded from config.yml in ingest_sgs().\n\nresolve_rm_site \u003c- function(labels,\n                            sites_path = here::here(\"reference/baseline_sites.csv\")) {\n  sites \u003c- utils::read.csv(sites_path, check.names = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n  sites \u003c- data.frame(\n    monitoring_location_id = sub(\"^KENAI_WQX-\", \"\", sites$MonitoringLocationIdentifier),\n    river_mile             = round(as.numeric(sites$river_mile), 2),\n    stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n  )\n  if (anyDuplicated(sites$river_mile)) {\n    stop(\"river_mile is not unique in \", sites_path, \"; resolve_rm_site() needs it to be.\",\n         call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  norm \u003c- gsub(\"_\", \" \", toupper(trimws(as.character(labels))))\n  # Lab typo: capital letter O for zero (\"RM O\")\n  norm \u003c- sub(\"\\\\bRM\\\\s*O\\\\b\", \"RM 0\", norm)\n\n  multi_site \u003c- grepl(\"&\", norm, fixed = TRUE)\n  has_rm     \u003c- grepl(\"RM\\\\s*[0-9]\", norm) & !multi_site\n  rm_num     \u003c- rep(NA_real_, length(norm))\n  rm_num[has_rm] \u003c- round(as.numeric(\n    sub(\".*RM\\\\s*([0-9]+(?:\\\\.[0-9]+)?).*\", \"\\\\1\", norm[has_rm], perl = TRUE)), 2)\n\n  is_trip  \u003c- grepl(\"TRIP\", norm)\n  is_blank \u003c- grepl(\"BLANK\", norm)\n\n  lab_qc_type \u003c- dplyr::case_when(\n    grepl(\"POSITIVE\", norm)             ~ \"LCS\",\n    is_blank & !has_rm & !is_trip       ~ \"MB\",\n    TRUE                                ~ NA_character_\n  )\n  sample_condition \u003c- dplyr::case_when(\n    !is.na(lab_qc_type)                 ~ NA_character_,\n    is_trip & has_rm                    ~ \"Trip Blank\",\n    is_blank & has_rm                   ~ \"Field Blank\",\n    grepl(\"DUP\", norm)                  ~ \"Field Duplicate\",\n    TRUE                                ~ NA_character_\n  )\n\n  id \u003c- sites$monitoring_location_id[match(rm_num, sites$river_mile)]\n  id[!is.na(lab_qc_type)] \u003c- NA_character_\n\n  unresolved \u003c- is.na(id) & is.na(lab_qc_type)\n  if (any(unresolved)) {\n    stop(\"Site labels not matched to a river mile in \", basename(sites_path), \": \",\n         paste(unique(labels[unresolved]), collapse = \"; \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  tibble::tibble(\n    label                  = as.character(labels),\n    monitoring_location_id = id,\n    sample_condition       = sample_condition,\n    lab_qc_type            = lab_qc_type\n  )\n}\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_019ffdqNCHFqWXqpJxC2dBxe"><script type="application/json">{"version":2,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"functions/ingest_fc.R","content":"# ingest_fc.R\n#\n# Reads one season of Soldotna Wastewater Treatment Plant (SWWTP) fecal co...[truncated, total 8390 chars]"},"caption":"functions/ingest_fc.R","captionLink":"functions/ingest_fc.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# ingest_fc.R\n#\n# Reads one season of Soldotna Wastewater Treatment Plant (SWWTP) fecal coliform\n# results and returns data that satisfy the `dat` contract (reference/dat_contract.md),\n# plus a lab QC table (method blanks and positive controls).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   config \u003c- yaml::read_yaml(here::here(\"config.yml\"))\n#   fc \u003c- ingest_fc(\"spring\", config)\n#   fc$dat      # field results (contract columns)\n#   fc$lab_qc   # BLANK / POSITIVE rows; never goes to the WQX export\n#\n# Year-specific settings come from config.yml (file path, sampling date, and the\n# receipt date/time from the chain of custody); nothing about a particular year is\n# hardcoded here. Fecal coliform is analyzed the day it is collected, so\n# run_date == collect_date; run_time is each row's \"Time In\".\n\ningest_fc \u003c- function(season,\n                      config,\n                      data_dir = here::here(\"data\"),\n                      sites_path = here::here(\"reference/baseline_sites.csv\")) {\n  stopifnot(season %in% c(\"spring\", \"summer\"))\n  suppressPackageStartupMessages({\n    library(dplyr); library(readxl); library(janitor)\n    library(stringr); library(lubridate); library(hms)\n  })\n\n  spec \u003c- config$files$fc[[season]]\n  if (is.null(spec)) stop(\"config.yml has no files$fc$\", season, call. = FALSE)\n  path \u003c- file.path(data_dir, spec$path)\n  if (!file.exists(path)) stop(\"FC file not found: \", path, call. = FALSE)\n\n  collect_date \u003c- as.Date(config$sampling[[paste0(season, \"_date\")]])\n  if (is.na(collect_date)) stop(\"config.yml sampling$\", season, \"_date is missing\", call. = FALSE)\n\n  # Read everything as text; locate the table by its header row\n  raw \u003c- read_excel(path, col_names = FALSE, col_types = \"text\", .name_repair = \"minimal\")\n  first_col \u003c- raw[[1]]\n  hdr_row \u003c- which(str_detect(first_col, regex(\"^\\\\s*Dish Number\", ignore_case = TRUE)))[1]\n  if (is.na(hdr_row)) {\n    stop(\"No 'Dish Number' header row found in \", basename(path),\n         \"; this file is not in the SWWTP fecal coliform format.\", call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  # Header block: \"DATE IN: MM-DD-YY\" must agree with the config collection date\n  header_text \u003c- unlist(raw[seq_len(hdr_row - 1), ], use.names = FALSE)\n  header_text \u003c- header_text[!is.na(header_text)]\n  parse_header_date \u003c- function(tag) {\n    hit \u003c- str_subset(header_text, regex(paste0(\"^\\\\s*\", tag, \"\\\\s*:\"), ignore_case = TRUE))[1]\n    if (is.na(hit)) return(as.Date(NA))\n    mdy(str_trim(str_remove(hit, regex(paste0(\"^\\\\s*\", tag, \"\\\\s*:\"), ignore_case = TRUE))),\n        quiet = TRUE)\n  }\n  date_in  \u003c- parse_header_date(\"DATE IN\")\n  date_out \u003c- parse_header_date(\"DATE OUT\")\n  if (is.na(date_in)) {\n    stop(\"No 'DATE IN' line found in \", basename(path), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  if (date_in != collect_date) {\n    stop(\"FC file \", basename(path), \" has DATE IN \", date_in,\n         \" but config.yml sampling$\", season, \"_date is \", collect_date,\n         \". Fecal coliform is run the day it is collected.\", call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  tbl \u003c- raw[(hdr_row + 1):nrow(raw), , drop = FALSE]\n  names(tbl) \u003c- make_clean_names(unlist(raw[hdr_row, ], use.names = FALSE))\n  needed \u003c- c(\"dish_number\", \"sample_location_rm\", \"ml\", \"time_sampled\", \"time_in\",\n              \"time_out\", \"colony_count_100m_l\")\n  if (!all(needed %in% names(tbl))) {\n    stop(\"FC file \", basename(path), \" is missing expected columns: \",\n         paste(setdiff(needed, names(tbl)), collapse = \", \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\n    filter(!is.na(sample_location_rm)) |>\n    mutate(sample_location_rm = str_trim(sample_location_rm))\n\n  # Sites not sampled this event are reported as N/A\n  not_sampled \u003c- str_detect(tbl$colony_count_100m_l, regex(\"^\\\\s*N/?A\\\\s*$\", ignore_case = TRUE)) |\n    is.na(tbl$colony_count_100m_l)\n  if (any(not_sampled)) {\n    message(\"FC \", season, \": dropped \", sum(not_sampled), \" row(s) with no result (N/A): \",\n            paste(tbl$sample_location_rm[not_sampled], collapse = \"; \"))\n  }\n  tbl \u003c- tbl[!not_sampled, ]\n\n  count \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(tbl$colony_count_100m_l))\n  if (anyNA(count)) {\n    stop(\"Non-numeric FC results in \", basename(path), \": \",\n         paste(unique(tbl$colony_count_100m_l[is.na(count)]), collapse = \"; \"),\n         call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  tbl$count \u003c- count\n\n  sites \u003c- resolve_rm_site(tbl$sample_location_rm, sites_path)\n\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\n    mutate(\n      monitoring_location_id = sites$monitoring_location_id,\n      sample_condition       = sites$sample_condition,\n      lab_qc_type            = sites$lab_qc_type,\n      time_sampled = parse_clock(time_sampled),\n      time_in      = parse_clock(time_in),\n      time_out     = parse_clock(time_out)\n    )\n\n  # Receipt comes from the chain of custody, which is not in the lab file\n  rec_date \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_date)) as.Date(NA) else as.Date(spec$rec_date)\n  rec_time \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_time)) as_hms(NA) else parse_clock_text(spec$rec_time)\n  if (is.na(rec_date) || is.na(rec_time)) {\n    warning(\"FC \", season, \": receipt date/time not set in config.yml files$fc$\", season,\n            \" (rec_date, rec_time). Copy them from the chain of custody. \",\n            \"rec_date and rec_time are NA; Q17 cannot evaluate FC hold times from receipt.\",\n            call. = FALSE)\n    rec_date \u003c- as.Date(NA)\n    rec_time \u003c- as_hms(NA)\n  }\n\n  common \u003c- tbl |>\n    mutate(\n      lab_name        = \"SWWTP\",\n      analyte         = \"Fecal Coliform\",\n      units           = \"cfu/100mL\",\n      analytical_method = \"9222D\",\n      epa_analysis_id = \"9222D\",\n      context_code    = \"APHA\",\n      loq             = 1,\n      run_date        = collect_date,\n      run_time        = time_in,\n      end_text        = paste0(\"analysis end = \",\n                               ifelse(is.na(date_out), \"\", format(date_out)), \" \",\n                               ifelse(is.na(time_out), \"\", format(time_out)))\n    )\n\n  lab_qc \u003c- common |>\n    filter(!is.na(lab_qc_type)) |>\n    transmute(\n      sample_type = lab_qc_type,\n      qc_group    = if_else(lab_qc_type == \"MB\", \"blank\", \"accuracy\"),\n      analyte, analytical_method,\n      result      = count,\n      resultflag  = if_else(count \u003c 1, \"U\", \"=\"),\n      repdl       = loq,\n      units,\n      amount_spiked = NA_real_, initial_conc = NA_real_, percent_recovered = NA_real_,\n      rec_limit_low = NA_real_, rec_limit_high = NA_real_, sample_rpd = NA_real_,\n      rpd_limit_low = NA_real_, rpd_limit_high = NA_real_,\n      anl_batch = NA_character_, prp_batch = NA_character_, sdg = NA_character_,\n      lab_sample_id = dish_number,\n      sample_id     = sample_location_rm,\n      collect_date  = collect_date,\n      run_date,\n      dissolved     = NA_character_,\n      lab_name,\n      season        = season\n    )\n\n  dat \u003c- common |>\n    filter(is.na(lab_qc_type)) |>\n    mutate(\n      # A zero count is a non-detect: no numeric result, flagged \"U\"\n      resultflag = if_else(count \u003c 1, \"U\", \"=\"),\n      result     = na_if(count, 0)\n    ) |>\n    transmute(\n      sample       = sample_location_rm,\n      lab_sample   = dish_number,\n      lab_name, analyte, result, units, resultflag, loq,\n      lod          = NA_real_,\n      collect_date = collect_date,\n      collect_time = time_sampled,\n      rec_date     = rec_date,\n      rec_time     = rec_time,\n      run_date, run_time,\n      analytical_method, epa_analysis_id, context_code,\n      monitoring_location_id,\n      note         = paste0(\"Analysis volume = \", ml, \" mL; \", end_text),\n      sample_type  = \"PS\",\n      sample_condition,\n      dissolved    = NA_character_,\n      result_sample_fraction = NA_character_\n    )\n\n  list(dat = dat, lab_qc = lab_qc)\n}\n\n# Clock values arrive either as an Excel fraction of a day (0.559 = 13:25) or as an\n# HHMM integer (1325). The format is detected per column; anything else stops.\nparse_clock \u003c- function(x) {\n  v \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(x))\n  ok \u003c- v[!is.na(v)]\n  if (!length(ok)) return(hms::as_hms(rep(NA_real_, length(x))))\n  if (all(ok >= 0 & ok \u003c 1)) {\n    hms::as_hms(round(v * 86400))\n  } else if (all(ok >= 0 & ok \u003c= 2359 & (ok %% 100) \u003c 60)) {\n    hms::as_hms(floor(v / 100) * 3600 + (v %% 100) * 60)\n  } else {\n    stop(\"Unrecognized clock format in values: \",\n         paste(utils::head(unique(x[!is.na(v)]), 5), collapse = \", \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n}\n\n# \"13:31\" or \"13:31:00\" from config.yml\nparse_clock_text \u003c- function(x) {\n  x \u003c- as.character(x)\n  if (grepl(\"^\\\\d{1,2}:\\\\d{2}$\", x)) x \u003c- paste0(x, \":00\")\n  hms::as_hms(x)\n}\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_01JeeGbBtKQCEoddbZAQRKsq"><script type="application/json">{"version":2,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\nsource(\"functions/resolve_rm_site.R\"); source(\"functions/ingest_fc.R\")\nconf...[truncated, total 1209 chars]","description":"Test helper and ingest_fc, compare to old dat_fc"},"description":"Test helper and ingest_fc, compare to old dat_fc","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\nsource(\"functions/resolve_rm_site.R\"); source(\"functions/ingest_fc.R\")\nconfig \u003c- yaml::read_yaml(\"config.yml\")\n\n# helper checks\nresolve_rm_site(c(\"RM 22 DUP\", \"RM22\", \"RM_10_DUP\", \"RM O\", \"RM 10.1\", \"BLANK\", \"POSITIVE CONTROL\", \"RM 18 FIELD BLANK\"))\ntry(resolve_rm_site(\"RM 99\"))\n\nsp \u003c- ingest_fc(\"spring\", config); su \u003c- ingest_fc(\"summer\", config)\nsapply(list(sp = sp$dat, su = su$dat, spq = sp$lab_qc, suq = su$lab_qc), nrow)\nnew_fc \u003c- bind_rows(sp$dat, su$dat)\nold_fc \u003c- dat_fc\ndim(new_fc); dim(old_fc)\n\n# compare results by site/date/condition\nkey \u003c- c(\"monitoring_location_id\", \"collect_date\", \"sample_condition\")\ncmp \u003c- full_join(\n  old_fc |> mutate(monitoring_location_id = as.character(monitoring_location_id)) |>\n    select(all_of(key), old_result = result, old_time = collect_time, old_flag = resultflag),\n  new_fc |> select(all_of(key), new_result = result, new_time = collect_time, new_flag = resultflag),\n  by = key)\nnrow(cmp); sum(is.na(cmp$old_flag) | is.na(cmp$new_flag))\ncmp |> filter(!(old_result == new_result | (old_result == 0 & is.na(new_result))) | old_time != new_time)\ncount(new_fc, units, analytical_method, resultflag)\nbind_rows(sp$lab_qc, su$lab_qc)\nhead(new_fc, 3)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

`resolve_rm_site()` and `ingest_fc()` are written, and `ingest_fc()` matches the existing `dat_fc` on every key I compared.

**Files**
- [functions/resolve_rm_site.R](functions/resolve_rm_site.R): the shared helper.
- [functions/ingest_fc.R](functions/ingest_fc.R): per-season FC ingest. It has no year-specific code.
- [config.yml](config.yml): the FC `skip` settings are removed. `rec_date` and `rec_time` fields are added and left blank, so 2025 receipt stays NA until the COC is available.

**Helper check.** It resolved `RM 22 DUP`, `RM22`, `RM_10_DUP`, `RM O`, `RM 10.1`, `BLANK`, `POSITIVE CONTROL` and `RM 18 FIELD BLANK` correctly. An unknown label (`RM 99`) stops with the label printed.

**Comparison with the old `dat_fc`**
- **Rows:** Spring 22 and summer 24, 46 in total, against 46 before. All 46 keys (site, date, condition) match between old and new.
- **Results and flags:** They agree everywhere. The 15 zero counts now have `result` NA with flag `U`, as designed.
- **Collect times:** They differ only at the 1e-12 second level, from rounding of Excel fractions. They are the same to the second.
- **Columns:** New `dat_fc` has 24 columns against 26 before, since `matrix` and `result_detection_condition` are dropped.
- **Expected changes:** Units are now `cfu/100mL` and `run_date` is the collection date, with `run_time` from `Time In`. `rec_date` and `rec_time` are NA, with a warning that tells the user to copy them from the COC. The note column now carries the volume and the analysis end time.
- **Not sampled:** The two spring sites with no result (`RM 6.5`, `RM 10.1`) were dropped with a message, as before.

**Lab QC.** `lab_qc` has 8 rows, 3 blanks and 1 positive control per season.

**A finding.** The summer positive control reads `TNTC` (too numerous to count), which is the expected outcome for a positive control. The old chunk dropped these rows before reading results, so it never hit this. `ingest_fc()` keeps the text in a new `result_text` column. For these rows `result` and `resultflag` are NA, so a later Q20 check can treat `TNTC` as a pass. A non-numeric result in a field sample still stops the run.

**Still to do**
- `2025.qmd` is not changed and still runs its own chunks, since it is a stepping stone and will be replaced.
- I haven't tested a `resolve_rm_site()` case for a `Trip Blank` label. It's logic I wrote but didn't exercise.

## Model of the repo (repeatedly emphasized by the user)

This repo is being developed into a **blank, robust template** that works for any year. It is not a repo that processes 2025 data.

- For each year the user will copy the template into a **new GitHub repo** and put only that year's data in `data/`. 2025 is just the test fixture used to refine the template.
- The template must (a) run the full QA/QC process for an annual dataset and (b) draft the preliminary report, based on the migrated 2025 example.
- Function code must contain nothing year-specific. Year-specific values live only in `config.yml`, `year_files/` and reference tables.
- At the end, the repo is blanked: remove 2025 raw data, outputs, `year_files/`, 2025 values in `config.yml`, and the legacy `project_year/2014-2024` and `other/input/<year>` folders. Leave placeholders, a `.gitkeep` in `data/`, and a README with a new-year checklist. Mark it as a GitHub template repository.

Decisions made this session:

| Question | Decision |
|---|---|
| What to do with the 2025 example at the end | Keep a **trimmed example folder in the template** (suggested name `example_2025/`; its contents and size are undecided, and raw lab files may not be wanted there) |
| Archive and fetch mechanics | **Delete.** Done. `functions/fetch_raw_data.R` and `functions/archive_year.R` are removed (still in git history), along with `data/.gitignore`, so no `.sha256` mechanism and no `raw-data-{year}` releases |
| Raw lab files | The per-year repo **commits** raw lab files (one year only). `data/` now holds 15 files (31 MB, all 2025), shown by git as untracked and not staged. The blank template keeps `data/` empty |
| Sequence | The "2025 first, then backwards" sequence and the promotion review are no longer relevant. Phases B, C and D stand, and there is a new final step that blanks the template |

## State of work (Phase B, per-lab ingest functions)

**`functions/ingest_sgs.R`** is done and verified (from before this session segment). `ingest_sgs(season, config, ...)` returns `list(dat, lab_qc)`.
- It routes the 14 lab QC sample types plus any `(surr)` analyte to `lab_qc`.
- It stops on an NA location ID, an NA `epa_analysis_id`, or non-WQX `mg/L` capitalization.
- `expand_trip_blanks()` reads `config.yml` `sgs: trip_blank_sites`.
- `dat` has 543 rows, matching the old `dat_sgs` minus 24 surrogates. `lab_qc` has 914 rows.

**`functions/resolve_rm_site.R`** is new and shared by FC, TSS and YSI.
- `resolve_rm_site(labels, sites_path)` parses the river-mile number from a lab label and matches it to `river_mile` in `reference/baseline_sites.csv` (unique across the 22 sites). It strips the `KENAI_WQX-` prefix and returns a bare `monitoring_location_id`.
- It reads the condition from the label text, never from site lists, because duplicate, field blank and trip blank locations vary by year and season.
  - `DUP` gives Field Duplicate.
  - `BLANK` plus an RM number gives Field Blank, or Trip Blank if `TRIP` is present.
  - `BLANK` with no RM number gives a lab method blank (`lab_qc_type` MB).
  - `POSITIVE` gives `lab_qc_type` LCS.
- It handles the `RM O` typo, `RM22`, and `RM_10_DUP`.
- It returns a tibble with `label`, `monitoring_location_id`, `sample_condition` and `lab_qc_type`.
- It stops, listing the labels, if a non-lab-QC label does not resolve.
- Labels containing `&` (multi-site trip blanks) are not resolved here; `ingest_sgs` handles them from config.
- Tested on eight labels, and `RM 99` stops correctly. **Not yet tested:** a `Trip Blank` label such as "RM1.5&6.5 trip blank" or "TRIP BLANK RM 10".

**`functions/ingest_fc.R`** is new. `ingest_fc(season, config, data_dir, sites_path)` returns `list(dat, lab_qc)`.
- **Reading the file:** It finds the header row by searching for "Dish Number", so there is no `skip` setting. It reads the file as text.
- **Date check:** It parses the `DATE IN` and `DATE OUT` header lines. It stops if `DATE IN` differs from `sampling.{season}_date` in config.
- **Clock values:** The `parse_clock()` helper detects the format per column (Excel fraction or HHMM) and stops on anything else.
- **Receipt:** `rec_date` and `rec_time` come from `config$files$fc[[season]]`, because they exist only in the chain of custody (COC), which is not in the repo. If they are missing, it warns and sets both to NA.
- **Fixed values:**
  - Units `cfu/100mL`
  - Method `9222D`
  - `epa_analysis_id` `9222D`
  - `context_code` `APHA`
  - `loq` 1
  - `lab_name` `SWWTP`
- **Results and dates:**
  - `run_date` equals `collect_date`; the user stated that FC is always run on the collection day.
  - `run_time` is each row's `Time In`.
  - Rows with a result of `N/A` are dropped with a message.
  - A zero count gives `result` NA and `resultflag` "U".
  - The `note` holds the analysis volume and the analysis end date and time.
- **Lab QC:** BLANK rows become MB with `qc_group` "blank"; POSITIVE rows become LCS with `qc_group` "accuracy". The FC `lab_qc` has an extra `result_text` column so a value like `TNTC` is kept.
- **Dropped columns:** `matrix` and `result_detection_condition`.
- **Comparison with the old `dat_fc` from `2025.qmd`:** All 46 rows (22 spring, 24 summer) match on site, date and condition, and results and flags agree. Collect times differ only at the 1e-12 second level. The columns went from 26 to 24. The `lab_qc` has 8 rows (3 blanks and 1 positive control per season).
- **TNTC finding:** The summer positive control reads `TNTC` (too numerous to count), which is expected for a positive control. The old chunk dropped these rows before reading results. In the new function a non-numeric result in a field sample still stops the run, and a non-numeric lab QC result is kept as `result_text` with `result` and `resultflag` NA.
- **To reconcile:** The SGS `lab_qc` has no `result_text` column, so the two `lab_qc` tables have different columns. This should be fixed when the lab QC tables are bound, or the column added to the contract.

**`config.yml` edits:**
- Header comment now says "Year configuration. Edit once per repo copy (one data year per repo)."
- The FC `skip` settings are removed. FC entries are now `{path, rec_date: ~, rec_time: ~}`, with a comment that receipt must be copied from the COC. The 2025 receipt is **left blank** until the COC is available.
- I did not carry the old 13:31 and 13:37 values into the 2025 config, because they are 2021 values from the report repo.
- The SGS, TSS and YSI entries are unchanged. TSS still has `skip` and `sheet`; YSI has `both: path`.

**Design decisions already settled (apply to the remaining ingest functions):**
- **FC units:** `cfu/100mL` with a capital L, matching WQP data from earlier years.
  - I could not read the WQX Unit domain list (EPA download URL gave 404, index page gave 403).
  - 2023 and 2021 scripts use `cfu/100ml`. Whether WQX Web matches case-insensitively is unknown; a trial import would settle it.
  - Nothing in `functions/` depends on `MPN`.
- **YSI:** `rec_date` and `rec_time` should be **NA**. These are in situ readings, and nothing went to a lab. The current `2025.qmd` copies the collect time into them, which is wrong.
- **Q17 (Phase C):**
  - A missing receipt must be reported as "not evaluable". The current code turns a missing `rec_time` into midnight.
  - YSI hold times are "not applicable".
  - A method with no holding time must not default to "Y" (pass).
  - `reference/wqx_templates/sample_holding_times.csv` has `9222D` at 6 hours.
  - For FC the 6-hour limit runs collection to analysis start, which the file records in `Time In`.
- **Per-year edits that remain** (go on the README new-year checklist):
  - New rows in `sgs_site_names_matching_table.xlsx` for new SGS duplicate and blank IDs.
  - `sgs: trip_blank_sites` in `config.yml`.
  - FC receipt date and time from the COC for each season.

Parses the river-mile number from a lab label and matches `river_mile` in `reference/baseline_sites.csv`; strips any `KENAI_WQX-` prefix. Condition comes from label text, never site lists: `DUP` = Field Duplicate; `BLANK` + RM number = Field Blank (Trip Blank if `TRIP`); `BLANK` without RM = lab MB; `POSITIVE` = LCS. Handles `RM O`, `RM22`, `RM_10_DUP`. Stops listing unresolved labels. `&` multi-site trip blanks are handled by `ingest_sgs` via config. Trip Blank labels not yet tested.\n+- **`ingest_fc.R`** `ingest_fc(season, config, data_dir, sites_path)`: finds the header row by searching \"Dish Number\" (no `skip` setting); stops if the file's `DATE IN` differs from config sampling date; `parse_clock()` detects Excel-fraction vs HHMM per column. Fixed values: units `cfu/100mL`, method and `epa_analysis_id` `9222D`, context `APHA`, `loq` 1, lab `SWWTP`. `run_date` = `collect_date` (user-stated: FC always runs on collection day); `run_time` = `Time In`. `rec_date`/`rec_time` come from `config$files$fc[[season]]` (COC only; warns and sets NA if missing; **2025 left blank until the COC is available**). `N/A` results dropped with a message; zero count gives `result` NA and flag \"U\". BLANK rows become MB (`qc_group` blank), POSITIVE become LCS (accuracy); `lab_qc` has an extra `result_text` column (summer positive control reads `TNTC`); a non-numeric result in a field sample stops the run. Verified against old `dat_fc`: all 46 rows match. **To reconcile:** SGS `lab_qc` lacks `result_text`.\n+\n+Settled rules for the remaining functions:\n+- FC units are `cfu/100mL` (matches WQP history; whether WQX Web accepts the case is unverified, a trial import would settle it).\n+- YSI `rec_date`/`rec_time` must be NA (in situ, no lab); the old chunk wrongly copies collect time.\n+- Q17 (Phase C): missing receipt is \"not evaluable\" (old code turns it into midnight); YSI is \"not applicable\"; a method with no holding time must not default to \"Y\". `sample_holding_times.csv` has `9222D` at 6 hours (collection to analysis start, i.e. `Time In`).\n+- Per-year edits for the README checklist: new rows in `sgs_site_names_matching_table.xlsx` for new SGS duplicate/blank IDs; `sgs: trip_blank_sites`; FC receipt date and time from the COC per season.\n+\n+Pending decision for `ingest_tss.R`: source of LOD/LOQ (recommended: `config.yml`; 2025 placeholders 0.5/1.0, 2023 was 0.31 mg/L), TSS receipt and run date, whether the file contains lab QC rows. References: `other/misc/report_repo_scripts/ingest_tss.R` and the TSS chunks in `2025.qmd` (about lines 573-800).\n+\n+## 2025.qmd Pipeline Structure (legacy inline version, being replaced)\n+\n ### Year Configuration chunk\n \n Sets `spring_sample_date = \"4/30/2025\"`, `summer_sample_date = \"7/23/2025\"`, paths, and `cfg` list. **This is the only block that changes between years.** All paths use `here::here()` so the document works correctly from `project_year/` regardless of the RStudio \"Evaluate chunks in directory\" setting.\n","language":"diff"}}</script></tool-call></div>

I updated [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md) to match the work done this session. I did not re-read the whole file afterward, only the edited spots.

- **Working model:** The file now describes the repo as a blank template that is copied into a new GitHub repo for each year, with only that year's data in it. Year-specific values live in `config.yml`, `year_files/` and reference tables. The end state is a blanked template with a trimmed example folder. The old "2025 first, then backwards" sequence and the promotion review are gone from the file.
- **Deleted tooling:** The archive, fetch, checksum and release mechanics are marked as removed.
- **Phases table:** Phase B is now "in progress", and `ingest_tss.R` is named as next. Phase E is now "blank the template".
- **Layout block:** It now lists the new functions, the `config.yml` contents, the committed `data/` folder, the book-type `_quarto.yml`, and a note that the README is stale.
- **New section "Per-Lab Ingest Functions":**
  - How `ingest_sgs`, `resolve_rm_site` and `ingest_fc` work.
  - The rules settled for the remaining functions: `cfu/100mL` units, YSI receipt NA, the Q17 fixes, and the per-year edits.
  - The pending TSS decision.
- **Known issues:** I added the YSI receipt bug, the two missing citations in `index.qmd`, and a list of stale docs (README, plan file, `reference/dat_contract.md`).
- **Plan-file note:** I added a line saying the plan file's archive content and item 13 are out of date, because the blank-template model supersedes them. The plan file itself is not revised.
- **Legacy heading:** I relabeled the old "2025.qmd Pipeline Structure" heading as the legacy inline version.

The README, the plan file and `reference/dat_contract.md` are still stale. I held back on them because they would change again as the ingest code settles.

move duplicate/blank status into separate column\r\n  mutate(sample_condition = case_when(\r\n    grepl(\"DUP\", sample_location) ~ \"Field Duplicate\")) %>%\r\n  # remove \"DUP\" designation from locations column\r\n  mutate(sample_location = str_replace(sample_location, \"_DUP\", \"\")) %>%\r\n  # replace \"O\" with zeros in location column (common typo)\r\n  mutate(sample_location = str_replace(sample_location, \"RM_O\", \"RM_0\")) %>%\r\n  # add units\r\n  # \"mg/L\" matches the WQX domain value and the other ingest scripts\r\n  mutate(units = \"mg/L\") %>%\r\n  rename(result = s_s_mg_l) %>%\r\n  transform(result = as.numeric(result)) %>%\r\n\r\n  # EPA analysis metadata from AWQMS template\r\n  mutate(epa_analysis_id = \"2540-D\",\r\n         analytical_method = \"SM21-2540-+D\",\r\n         context_code = \"APHA\",\r\n         note = \"\") %>%\r\n\r\n  # remove weight columns not needed for WQX export\r\n  select(-dried_wt, -paper_wt, -tare_wt_kg, -ml) %>%\r\n\r\n  # modify date/time formats\r\n  mutate(collect_date = as.character(paste(field_sample_date, sample_time)),\r\n         run_date_time = as.character(paste(date_of_analysis, analysis_time)),\r\n         .keep = \"unused\") %>%\r\n  mutate(collect_time = as_hms(as.POSIXct(collect_date))) %>%\r\n  mutate(collect_date = date(as.POSIXct(collect_date)),\r\n         run_time = as_hms(ymd_hms(run_date_time)),\r\n         run_date = date(ymd_hms(run_date_time)),\r\n         .keep = \"unused\") %>%\r\n\r\n  rename(analyst = signature) %>%\r\n\r\n  mutate(lab_sample = \"\",\r\n         matrix = \"Water\",\r\n         analyte = \"Total suspended solids\",\r\n         loq = 1.0,    # reporting limit\r\n         lod = 0.31,   # method detection limit\r\n         lab_name = \"Soldotna Wastewater Treatment Plant, Soldotna, Alaska\")\r\n\r\n# assign sample_type\r\nswwtp_tss %\u003c>%\r\n  mutate(sample_type = case_when(\r\n    sample_condition == \"Lab Blank\" ~ \"MB\",\r\n    sample_condition == \"Positive Control\" ~ \"LCS\",\r\n    TRUE ~ \"PS\"))\r\n\r\n# join site names\r\nswwtp_tss_sitenames \u003c- data.frame(unique(swwtp_tss$sample_location))\r\nunlink(\"other/input/wqx_templates/swwtp_tss_sitenames.csv\")\r\nwrite.csv(swwtp_tss_sitenames, \"other/input/wqx_templates/swwtp_tss_sitenames.csv\", row.names = FALSE)\r\nswwtp_tss_sitenames \u003c- read_excel(\"other/input/wqx_templates/swwtp_tss_site_names_matching_table_manual_edit.xlsx\")\r\nswwtp_tss \u003c- left_join(swwtp_tss, swwtp_tss_sitenames) %>%\r\n  clean_names() %>%\r\n  rename(sample = sample_location)\r\n\r\n# assign result flags\r\n# J: below LOQ but above LOD (0.31); U: below LOD; =: detected\r\nswwtp_tss %\u003c>%\r\n  mutate(resultflag = case_when(\r\n    result \u003c 1 & result > 0.31 ~ \"J\",\r\n    result \u003c 0.31 ~ \"U\",\r\n    TRUE ~ \"=\"))\r\n\r\n# modify non-detect values from \"0\" to \"NA\"\r\nswwtp_tss %\u003c>%\r\n  mutate(result1 = na_if(result, 0)) %>%\r\n  select(-result) %>%\r\n  rename(result = result1)\r\n\r\n# ── Lab QA/QC export (currently disabled due to SWWTP reporting gap) ──────────\r\n# SWWTP did not report required lab QA results for TSS in 2021 or 2022.\r\n# QAPP requires: lab blank, lab duplicate sample, external QC check sample.\r\n# Reinstate this block when SWWTP resumes QA reporting. See session_log.md.\r\n#\r\n# swwtp_tss_qaqc \u003c- swwtp_tss %>%\r\n#   filter(sample_type %in% c(\"MB\",\"LCS\"))\r\n# write.csv(swwtp_tss_qaqc,\r\n#            file.path(output_qaqc_dir, \"tss_qaqc_dat.csv\"), row.names = FALSE)\r\n# rm(swwtp_tss_qaqc)\r\n\r\n# bind TSS into overall dataset\r\ndat \u003c- bind_rows(dat, swwtp_tss)\r\nrm(swwtp_tss_sitenames, swwtp_tss)\r\n"},"elapsedMs":14}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><thinking id="thinking-1793232633381-oa9f6">
I notice the TSS files actually contain blank rows (sample_type MB) with lab method blank results like 0.33 mg/L, four per season — meaning lab QC data does exist here, unlike the 2021/22 gap I'd assumed. I also see the spring file is missing Date_of_Analysis/Time columns that the summer file has, so the schemas aren't consistent across seasons.

Looking closer, spring RM 6.5 and RM 10.1 have no Sample_Time, no Dried_WT, and result 0 — these look like sites that weren't actually sampled rather than true zero results. That's concerning because the current code would treat a 0 result as below the LOD and flag it "U" (non-detect), when really these rows should just be dropped for missing data. I want to flag this as a question rather than dig deeper right now, and I'm also noting some odd Tare_WT values in the summer file's early rows worth a second look.

<div><thinking id="thinking-1793232643242-xi620">
I'm checking the summer file's date and time fields, and noting the RM 1.5 result of 763 mg/L seems high but plausible given tidal variation compared to spring's 493 — not something I need to flag.

Now focusing on the LOD/LOQ decision: the QAPP table specifies TSS LOD of 0.31 mg/L and LOQ of 1.0 mg/L with ±25% recovery criteria, which means the current placeholder value of 0.5 is incorrect. I should recommend updating the config with the correct LOD/LOQ values under the tss section, citing the QAPP source, while flagging other observations (missing spring analysis dates, zero-weight rows treated as non-detects, lab blank handling) as upcoming decisions rather than addressing them now. I'll also need to define the flagging logic — results below LOD get marked "U" with NA, and results between LOD and LOQ get marked "J".


</thinking><span data-thinking-duration-ms="9298"></span></div>

I've read the report repo's `ingest_tss.R`, the TSS chunks in `2025.qmd` and the two raw files. I haven't written any code. This first decision is about LOD and LOQ.

**Proposal: LOD and LOQ come from `config.yml`.**

The function would read them from a new block, next to the existing `sgs:` block:

```yaml
tss:
  lod: 0.31   # mg/L, QAPP v3 Table 5 (SM21-2540-+D)
  loq: 1.0    # mg/L
```

It would stop with an error if either value is missing. The flag logic would stay as it is now: below the LOD gives `U` with a blank result, between the LOD and LOQ gives `J`, and anything else gives `=`. Each year's repo sets these values once, so the function code holds no numbers.

**The QAPP contradicts the `2025.qmd` placeholders.** Table 5 of `QAPP-v3-2023-with-Addendum-April-2024.md` lists TSS (SM21-2540-+D) with LOD 0.31 mg/L and LOQ 1.0 mg/L, plus RPD ≤5 and ±25% recovery. The 2025 chunk uses 0.5 as the LOD, so any 2025 result between 0.31 and 0.5 is currently flagged `U` when the QAPP would call it `J`. The 2023 value and the report repo's script both use 0.31. I'd put 0.31 and 1.0 in the 2025 config, with the QAPP table cited in the comment. The one open point is whether SWWTP's 2025 lab report states different limits. I can't check that from the files.

**What I found in the raw files** (these feed the later decisions):
- **Lab blanks are in the files.** Both seasons have four `MB` rows each (`BLANK 1` to `BLANK 4`), and the old chunk filters them out with `sample_type == "PS"`. LOD and LOQ are in `config.yml` now, as a new `tss:` block at the bottom with `lod: 0.31` and `loq: 1.0`. I've also noted the sites that weren't visited. Any TSS row with no dried weight and no sample time gets dropped as "not sampled", with a message listing the site, instead of being flagged as a non-detect. A real zero (weights recorded, result below the LOD) still becomes `U`. I'd also rather the function stop than guess if a row has weights but no time, or the reverse.

**Lab blank rows: proposal.** Both seasons have four `MB` rows each, labelled `BLANK 1` to `BLANK 4`. This is what they hold:

| Season | Blank results (mg/L) |
|---|---|
| Spring | 0.33, 0, 0.33, 0 |
| Summer | 0, 0, 0, 0 |

The proposal is to treat them the way `ingest_fc` treats its blanks.
- **Routing:** `ingest_tss` returns `list(dat, lab_qc)`. Rows with `sample_type == "MB"` go to `lab_qc` and never reach `dat` or the WQX export. Any `sample_type` other than `PS` or `MB` (for example `LCS` or `DUP` in a future year) would stop the run with the unexpected value listed, rather than being silently dropped or silently treated as field samples. Labelling is by the file's `sample_type` column, with `resolve_rm_site` used only for field sample labels. A `BLANK n` label would not be treated as a site.
- **Columns:** The same layout as the FC `lab_qc` (`sample_type`, `qc_group` = "blank", `analyte`, `analytical_method`, `result`, `resultflag`, `repdl` = the LOQ, `units`, `lab_sample_id`, `sample_id` = the blank label, `collect_date`, `run_date`, `lab_name`, `season`, plus the empty accuracy and precision columns). The `result_text` column isn't needed for TSS.
- **Result values:** `lab_qc` keeps the result as reported (0 stays 0), with `resultflag` set from the limits. 0 gives `U`, and 0.33 gives `J` because it sits between the LOD (0.31) and the LOQ (1.0). Field samples in `dat` keep the existing rule of a blank result for non-detects. A blank result of 0.33 is above the LOD, so the Phase C blank check should look at it, but that is not the ingest function's job.
- **Dates for blanks:** Blanks have no sample time. `collect_date` would be the season's config date, as it is for FC blanks. I'll come back to `run_date` with the receipt and run date decision.

**One thing for the next decision.** The spring file has no analysis date column, and its `Field_Sample_Date` reads 5/1/2025, a day after the 4/30 sampling date. That looks like the analysis date under the wrong heading (the old `2025.qmd` notes say the same). The summer file has a proper `Date_of_Analysis` of 7/25/2025 at 14:00. So TSS `run_date` needs a rule that works for both layouts, and TSS receipt is a COC value like FC.

**Decision for you:** are you happy with routing all `MB` rows to `lab_qc` in the FC layout, stopping on any other `sample_type`, and keeping blank results as reported?

Confirmed on both points: spring collection was 4/30/2025, which already matches `config.yml`. TSS rows with `sample_type == "MB"` will route to `lab_qc` in the FC layout. Because the spring file's `Field_Sample_Date` column reads 5/1, the function will take `collect_date` from config and not read that column.

**Where the run date and time are in the files**

| | Tidy sheet (`Updated_Formatting`) | Original wide sheet header |
|---|---|---|
| Spring (`.xlsx`) | no analysis columns; `Field_Sample_Date` holds 5/1, the analysis date, under the wrong heading | `DATE OF ANALYSIS 05-01-25`, `TIME 13:58` (in the original `.xls`, `KRWF TSS MONITORING 05-01-25.xls`) |
| Summer (`.xls`) | `Date_of_Analysis` 7/25/2025 and `Time` 14:00 on every row | `DATE OF ANALYSIS 07-25-25`, `TIME 1400` (`Original_Formatting` sheet) |

The two seasons lay out the same information differently, so no single column rule reads both. The reliable constant is that the lab writes the date and time of analysis in the header of the original sheet. That header is also where the 2025.qmd placeholder of `5/1/2025` at midnight came from.

**Proposal: run date and time come from `config.yml`, per season.** They'd go next to the file path, the same way FC receipt does:

```yaml
tss:
  spring: {path: ..., sheet: "Updated_Formatting",
           run_date: "2025-05-01", run_time: "13:58", rec_date: ~, rec_time: ~}
  summer: {path: ..., run_date: "2025-07-25", run_time: "14:00", rec_date: ~, rec_time: ~}
```

- **If the summer tidy sheet has `Date_of_Analysis`:** the function checks it against config and stops on a mismatch, as FC does with `DATE IN`. The spring file has no such column, so there is no check there.
- **If `run_date` or `run_time` is missing from config:** the function stops, because analysis timing is needed for the holding-time check. This is stricter than the receipt fields, which only warn.
- **Applies to all rows:** SWWTP weighs and filters the whole batch in one session. The same run date and time apply to every field sample and every lab blank.
- **Header search, no `skip`:** The function would find its header row by searching for `Sample_Location`, so the `skip` setting leaves the config, as with FC.
- **File layout:** The existing `tss:` block with `lod` and `loq` stays at the top level, and per-season paths and timing sit under `files: tss:`.

**Receipt** has the same shape (`rec_date` and `rec_time` in config, warn and set to NA if blank). There is no SWWTP chain of custody in `data/`, only the SGS ones, so for 2025 it would stay blank for now, as with FC.

**Decision for you:** are you happy with run date and time coming from config per season (with the summer cross-check and a hard stop if missing), using 5/1/2025 at 13:58 and 7/25/2025 at 14:00?


<span class="suggestion">Approve TSS run date and time from config per season, then settle TSS receipt and the remaining design points.</span>

Confirmed: for TSS the collection date and run date differ, unlike FC. The 2025 values:

| Season | Collected | Analyzed | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|
| Spring | 4/30/2025 | 5/1/2025 at 13:58 | about 1 day |
| Summer | 7/23/2025 | 7/25/2025 at 14:00 | about 2 days |

So TSS `collect_date` and `run_date` are separate fields, and the function never sets one from the other. The holding time for method `2540-D` in `sample_holding_times.csv` is 7 days (168 hours), and the QAPP says the same. Both seasons are well inside it.

**Config changes made.** `config.yml` now has `run_date`, `run_time`, `rec_date` and `rec_time` on each TSS season, and the old `skip` settings are gone. The 2025 run values are the ones you approved, and the header comment explains where to copy each from. The `tss:` block with `lod: 0.31` and `loq: 1.0` is still at the bottom.

**TSS receipt, proposal.** It works the same way as FC:
- `rec_date` and `rec_time` are read from config and are optional.
- If either is blank, the function warns and sets both to NA.
- For 2025 they stay blank, because there is no SWWTP chain of custody in `data/`.
- The TSS holding-time check runs from collection to the start of analysis (7 days), as FC does. A blank receipt doesn't stop Q17 from being evaluated for TSS. It would only affect any receipt-based checks.

**Remaining design points.** These are mechanical, and I'll build them in unless you object:
- **Dropped rows:** Field samples with no dried weight and no sample time are dropped as "not sampled", with a message.
- **`lab_name`:** Set to `SWWTP`, as in FC and the current chunk.
- **Fixed values:**
  - `analyte` is "Total suspended solids".
  - `units` is `mg/L`.
  - `context_code` is `APHA`.
  - `result_sample_fraction` is left NA, so `format_wqx.R` assigns "Suspended".
- **Result rules:** a real zero gives a blank result with flag `U` for field samples, and `J` is for results from 0.31 up to (but not including) 1.0 mg/L. A non-numeric result in a field sample stops the run.
- **Notes and lab sample:** `lab_sample` is the row's label. `note` holds the sample volume (`ML`) in mL, as FC does with its analysis volume.
- **Dropped columns:** `matrix` and `result_detection_condition` are no longer produced.

**One decision for you: the method identifier.** The three sources disagree on how to name TSS:

| Source | `analytical_method` | `epa_analysis_id` |
|---|---|---|
| `2025.qmd` (current chunk) | `2540-D` | `2540-D` |
| Report repo's `ingest_tss.R` (AWQMS template) | `SM21-2540-+D` | `2540-D` |
| QAPP Table 5 | `SM21-2540-+D` | not given |

`sample_holding_times.csv` keys the TSS hold on `2540-D`, so changing `analytical_method` would break the Q17 join unless the join uses `epa_analysis_id`. The earlier years on WQP are probably the best evidence of what EPA accepts. Which should the function emit for `analytical_method`: `2540-D` as in 2025.qmd, or `SM21-2540-+D` as in the report repo? I can't tell from here which one WQX Web accepted in past uploads. If you don't remember, a quick query of 2021-2023 TSS rows on WQP would tell us.


<span class="suggestion">Check which TSS analytical method identifier earlier years used on WQP (2021-2023), then settle the method ID.</span>

The method ID is settled: `2540-D`. I compared the files. The 2021 results file (`other/output/example_output/results_activities_2021.csv`) and the 2025 export both carry `Result Analytical Method ID = 2540-D` with context `APHA`. `SM21-2540-+D` appears only in the QAPP and the report repo's script. Using `2540-D` for both `analytical_method` and `epa_analysis_id` also keeps the holding-time join and the `format_wqx.R` fraction rule (which matches "2540") working. Your note that the method didn't change between 2021 and 2025 fits this.

**Final design for `functions/ingest_tss.R`:**

`ingest_tss(season, config, data_dir, sites_path)` returns `list(dat, lab_qc)`.

1. **Config in:**
   - Reads the sampling date from `sampling.{season}_date` (this is `collect_date`).
   - Reads path, sheet, `run_date`, `run_time`, `rec_date` and `rec_time` from `files.tss.{season}`.
   - Reads `lod` and `loq` from the `tss:` block.
   - Stops if the LOD, LOQ, `run_date` or `run_time` is missing.
   - Warns and sets NA if the receipt is blank.
2. **Read the sheet:** Finds the header row by searching for `Sample_Location`, and reads everything as text before parsing.
   - **Time parsing:** `Sample_Time` is parsed with the FC `parse_clock()` approach (Excel fraction or HHMM), so the 1899-12-31 artifact doesn't matter.
   - **Summer cross-check:** If `Date_of_Analysis` exists (summer), it is checked against config `run_date`.
3. **Classify rows:**
   - `sample_type == "MB"` goes to `lab_qc`.
   - `PS` rows are field samples.
   - Anything else stops the run with the value listed.
   - Field sample labels go through `resolve_rm_site` (this handles `RM O`, `RM22`, `RM 10_DUP` and `RM_23_DUP`). Field samples with no dried weight and no sample time are dropped as not sampled, with a message listing the sites.
   - A row with only one of the two stops the run.
4. **Results:**
   - `result` is a number, flagged with the config LOD and LOQ.
   - In `dat`, a real zero or a value below the LOD becomes a blank result with flag `U`.
   - A value from the LOD up to but not including the LOQ is flagged `J`.
   - In `lab_qc`, the blank result is kept as reported.
5. **Fixed values:**
   - `analyte` is "Total suspended solids" and `units` is `mg/L`.
   - `analytical_method` and `epa_analysis_id` are `2540-D`, with context `APHA`.
   - `lab_name` is `SWWTP`.
   - `result_sample_fraction` is left NA and `format_wqx.R` sets "Suspended".
   - `note` records the sample volume in mL.
6. **Columns:** `dat` has the 24 contract columns and no `matrix` or `result_detection_condition`. `lab_qc` has the same layout as the FC `lab_qc`.
7. **Verification once written:** Compare the field sample rows against the old `dat_tss`, expecting differences only from the LOD change (0.5 to 0.31), `mg/L`, dropped columns, the removed not-sampled rows, and the blanks moving to `lab_qc`.

**Expected check results:** Spring should give 25 field rows minus the 2 not-sampled sites, so 23, plus 4 blanks. Summer should give 22 field rows or fewer, depending on the sheet, plus 4 blanks. I'll confirm the counts against the files once the function runs.

Shall I go ahead and write it?

Fecal coliform is analyzed the day it is collected, so\r\n# run_date == collect_date; run_time is each row's \"Time In\".\r\n\r\ningest_fc \u003c- function(season,\r\n                      config,\r\n                      data_dir = here::here(\"data\"),\r\n                      sites_path = here::here(\"reference/baseline_sites.csv\")) {\r\n  stopifnot(season %in% c(\"spring\", \"summer\"))\r\n  suppressPackageStartupMessages({\r\n    library(dplyr); library(readxl); library(janitor)\r\n    library(stringr); library(lubridate); library(hms)\r\n  })\r\n\r\n  spec \u003c- config$files$fc[[season]]\r\n  if (is.null(spec)) stop(\"config.yml has no files$fc$\", season, call. = FALSE)\r\n  path \u003c- file.path(data_dir, spec$path)\r\n  if (!file.exists(path)) stop(\"FC file not found: \", path, call. = FALSE)\r\n\r\n  collect_date \u003c- as.Date(config$sampling[[paste0(season, \"_date\")]])\r\n  if (is.na(collect_date)) stop(\"config.yml sampling$\", season, \"_date is missing\", call. = FALSE)\r\n\r\n  # Read everything as text; locate the table by its header row\r\n  raw \u003c- read_excel(path, col_names = FALSE, col_types = \"text\", .name_repair = \"minimal\")\r\n  first_col \u003c- raw[[1]]\r\n  hdr_row \u003c- which(str_detect(first_col, regex(\"^\\\\s*Dish Number\", ignore_case = TRUE)))[1]\r\n  if (is.na(hdr_row)) {\r\n    stop(\"No 'Dish Number' header row found in \", basename(path),\r\n         \"; this file is not in the SWWTP fecal coliform format.\", call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  # Header block: \"DATE IN: MM-DD-YY\" must agree with the config collection date\r\n  header_text \u003c- unlist(raw[seq_len(hdr_row - 1), ], use.names = FALSE)\r\n  header_text \u003c- header_text[!is.na(header_text)]\r\n  parse_header_date \u003c- function(tag) {\r\n    hit \u003c- str_subset(header_text, regex(paste0(\"^\\\\s*\", tag, \"\\\\s*:\"), ignore_case = TRUE))[1]\r\n    if (is.na(hit)) return(as.Date(NA))\r\n    mdy(str_trim(str_remove(hit, regex(paste0(\"^\\\\s*\", tag, \"\\\\s*:\"), ignore_case = TRUE))),\r\n        quiet = TRUE)\r\n  }\r\n  date_in  \u003c- parse_header_date(\"DATE IN\")\r\n  date_out \u003c- parse_header_date(\"DATE OUT\")\r\n  if (is.na(date_in)) {\r\n    stop(\"No 'DATE IN' line found in \", basename(path), call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n  if (date_in != collect_date) {\r\n    stop(\"FC file \", basename(path), \" has DATE IN \", date_in,\r\n         \" but config.yml sampling$\", season, \"_date is \", collect_date,\r\n         \". Fecal coliform is run the day it is collected.\", call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  tbl \u003c- raw[(hdr_row + 1):nrow(raw), , drop = FALSE]\r\n  names(tbl) \u003c- make_clean_names(unlist(raw[hdr_row, ], use.names = FALSE))\r\n  needed \u003c- c(\"dish_number\", \"sample_location_rm\", \"ml\", \"time_sampled\", \"time_in\",\r\n              \"time_out\", \"colony_count_100m_l\")\r\n  if (!all(needed %in% names(tbl))) {\r\n    stop(\"FC file \", basename(path), \" is missing expected columns: \",\r\n         paste(setdiff(needed, names(tbl)), collapse = \", \"), call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\r\n    filter(!is.na(sample_location_rm)) |>\r\n    mutate(sample_location_rm = str_trim(sample_location_rm))\r\n\r\n  # Sites not sampled this event are reported as N/A\r\n  not_sampled \u003c- str_detect(tbl$colony_count_100m_l, regex(\"^\\\\s*N/?A\\\\s*$\", ignore_case = TRUE)) |\r\n    is.na(tbl$colony_count_100m_l)\r\n  if (any(not_sampled)) {\r\n    message(\"FC \", season, \": dropped \", sum(not_sampled), \" row(s) with no result (N/A): \",\r\n            paste(tbl$sample_location_rm[not_sampled], collapse = \"; \"))\r\n  }\r\n  tbl \u003c- tbl[!not_sampled, ]\r\n\r\n  sites \u003c- resolve_rm_site(tbl$sample_location_rm, sites_path)\r\n\r\n  # Lab QC rows may be text (a positive control is typically \"TNTC\"); field results may not\r\n  tbl$result_text \u003c- tbl$colony_count_100m_l\r\n  tbl$count \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(tbl$colony_count_100m_l))\r\n  bad \u003c- is.na(tbl$count) & is.na(sites$lab_qc_type)\r\n  if (any(bad)) {\r\n    stop(\"Non-numeric FC field results in \", basename(path), \": \",\r\n         paste(unique(tbl$result_text[bad]), collapse = \"; \"), call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\r\n    mutate(\r\n      monitoring_location_id = sites$monitoring_location_id,\r\n      sample_condition       = sites$sample_condition,\r\n      lab_qc_type            = sites$lab_qc_type,\r\n      time_sampled = parse_clock(time_sampled),\r\n      time_in      = parse_clock(time_in),\r\n      time_out     = parse_clock(time_out)\r\n    )\r\n\r\n  # Receipt comes from the chain of custody, which is not in the lab file\r\n  rec_date \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_date)) as.Date(NA) else as.Date(spec$rec_date)\r\n  rec_time \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_time)) as_hms(NA) else parse_clock_text(spec$rec_time)\r\n  if (is.na(rec_date) || is.na(rec_time)) {\r\n    warning(\"FC \", season, \": receipt date/time not set in config.yml files$fc$\", season,\r\n            \" (rec_date, rec_time). <div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01AuAEviNQ5J16pYqdSSBMVr"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 3.1KB, 76 lines, showing lines 1-76]\n1\t# resolve_rm_site.R\n2\t#\n3\t# Maps lab site la...[truncated, total 3477 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 3.1KB, 76 lines, showing lines 1-76]\n# resolve_rm_site.R\n#\n# Maps lab site labels such as \"RM 22\", \"RM22 DUP\", \"RM_10_DUP\", \"RM O\" to a\n# bare monitoring_location_id by parsing the river-mile number and matching it to\n# `river_mile` in reference/baseline_sites.csv (unique across the 22 sites).\n#\n# Sample condition is read from the label text, never from a list of sites, because\n# duplicate, field blank and trip blank locations change every year and season:\n#   \"DUP\"                      -> Field Duplicate\n#   \"BLANK\" with an RM number  -> Field Blank (or Trip Blank when \"TRIP\" is present)\n#   \"BLANK\" with no RM number  -> lab method blank, lab_qc_type \"MB\"\n#   \"POSITIVE\"                 -> lab positive control, lab_qc_type \"LCS\"\n#\n# Returns a tibble: label, monitoring_location_id (chr, no KENAI_WQX- prefix),\n# sample_condition, lab_qc_type. Stops, listing the labels, if a label that is not a\n# lab QC row cannot be matched to a site.\n#\n# Multi-site labels (containing \"&\", e.g. SGS \"RM1.5&6.5 trip blank\") are not\n# resolved here; they are expanded from config.yml in ingest_sgs().\n\nresolve_rm_site \u003c- function(labels,\n                            sites_path = here::here(\"reference/baseline_sites.csv\")) {\n  sites \u003c- utils::read.csv(sites_path, check.names = FALSE, stringsAsFactors = FALSE)\n  sites \u003c- data.frame(\n    monitoring_location_id = sub(\"^KENAI_WQX-\", \"\", sites$MonitoringLocationIdentifier),\n    river_mile             = round(as.numeric(sites$river_mile), 2),\n    stringsAsFactors = FALSE\n  )\n  if (anyDuplicated(sites$river_mile)) {\n    stop(\"river_mile is not unique in \", sites_path, \"; resolve_rm_site() needs it to be.\",\n         call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  norm \u003c- gsub(\"_\", \" \", toupper(trimws(as.character(labels))))\n  # Lab typo: capital letter O for zero (\"RM O\")\n  norm \u003c- sub(\"\\\\bRM\\\\s*O\\\\b\", \"RM 0\", norm)\n\n  multi_site \u003c- grepl(\"&\", norm, fixed = TRUE)\n  has_rm     \u003c- grepl(\"RM\\\\s*[0-9]\", norm) & !multi_site\n  rm_num     \u003c- rep(NA_real_, length(norm))\n  rm_num[has_rm] \u003c- round(as.numeric(\n    sub(\".*RM\\\\s*([0-9]+(?:\\\\.[0-9]+)?).*\", \"\\\\1\", norm[has_rm], perl = TRUE)), 2)\n\n  is_trip  \u003c- grepl(\"TRIP\", norm)\n  is_blank \u003c- grepl(\"BLANK\", norm)\n\n  lab_qc_type \u003c- dplyr::case_when(\n    grepl(\"POSITIVE\", norm)             ~ \"LCS\",\n    is_blank & !has_rm & !is_trip       ~ \"MB\",\n    TRUE                                ~ NA_character_\n  )\n  sample_condition \u003c- dplyr::case_when(\n    !is.na(lab_qc_type)                 ~ NA_character_,\n    is_trip & has_rm                    ~ \"Trip Blank\",\n    is_blank & has_rm                   ~ \"Field Blank\",\n    grepl(\"DUP\", norm)                  ~ \"Field Duplicate\",\n    TRUE                                ~ NA_character_\n  )\n\n  id \u003c- sites$monitoring_location_id[match(rm_num, sites$river_mile)]\n  id[!is.na(lab_qc_type)] \u003c- NA_character_\n\n  unresolved \u003c- is.na(id) & is.na(lab_qc_type)\n  if (any(unresolved)) {\n    stop(\"Site labels not matched to a river mile in \", basename(sites_path), \": \",\n         paste(unique(labels[unresolved]), collapse = \"; \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  tibble::tibble(\n    label                  = as.character(labels),\n    monitoring_location_id = id,\n    sample_condition       = sample_condition,\n    lab_qc_type            = lab_qc_type\n  )\n}\n"},"elapsedMs":88}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><tool-result call-id="toolu_01LS2kZqzNAJihd6RbHhnmjg"><script type="application/json">{"version":1,"status":"success","result":{"type":"text","value":"[File: 8.6KB, 210 lines, showing lines 185-210]\n185\t  list(dat = dat, lab_qc = lab_qc)\r\n18...[truncated, total 1044 chars]"},"resultPreview":{"type":"code","value":"[File: 8.6KB, 210 lines, showing lines 185-210]\n  list(dat = dat, lab_qc = lab_qc)\r\n}\r\n\r\n# Clock values arrive either as an Excel fraction of a day (0.559 = 13:25) or as an\r\n# HHMM integer (1325). The format is detected per column; anything else stops.\r\nparse_clock \u003c- function(x) {\r\n  v \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(x))\r\n  ok \u003c- v[!is.na(v)]\r\n  if (!length(ok)) return(hms::as_hms(rep(NA_real_, length(x))))\r\n  if (all(ok >= 0 & ok \u003c 1)) {\r\n    hms::as_hms(round(v * 86400))\r\n  } else if (all(ok >= 0 & ok \u003c= 2359 & (ok %% 100) \u003c 60)) {\r\n    hms::as_hms(floor(v / 100) * 3600 + (v %% 100) * 60)\r\n  } else {\r\n    stop(\"Unrecognized clock format in values: \",\r\n         paste(utils::head(unique(x[!is.na(v)]), 5), collapse = \", \"), call. = FALSE)\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n\r\n# \"13:31\" or \"13:31:00\" from config.yml\r\nparse_clock_text \u003c- function(x) {\r\n  x \u003c- as.character(x)\r\n  if (grepl(\"^\\\\d{1,2}:\\\\d{2}$\", x)) x \u003c- paste0(x, \":00\")\r\n  hms::as_hms(x)\r\n}\r\n"},"elapsedMs":45}</script></tool-result></div>

<div><thinking id="thinking-1793233750940-647a1">
The old dat_tss has 48 rows. Since parse_clock lives in ingest_fc.R, I'm weighing whether ingest_tss should depend on it directly, extract it to a shared helpers file, or just check for its existence at runtime—leaning toward a small refactor to share the helper cleanly.

Given the risk of multi-line edits failing on some files, I'll go with the lower-risk option: have ingest_tss.R source ingest_fc.R as a guard if parse_clock isn't already defined, and leave the cleaner relocation as an optional future step. For the summer file, I need to handle the Excel date serial numbers and time fractions as text, and account for the extra title row before the header when searching for "Sample_Location".

With col_names set to FALSE and unnamed/blank headers, cleaning produces generic names I can work around since I only need specific columns to select — duplicates like "x" and "x_2" from NA headers are fine as long as I target the right ones. I'm now working through the "not sampled" logic for dedupe: rows with missing dried weight and sample time get a result of "0", while rows with a sample time but missing dried weight need separate handling.

For flagging, I'm computing numeric results from the S.S. mg/L column and applying lab qualifiers: values below the detection limit get flagged "U" and nulled out, values below the quantitation limit get "J", otherwise "=". I'm cross-checking a few specific spring sample values against the LOD/LOQ thresholds to make sure the floating-point comparisons land correctly.

I'm also weighing whether to round results given the floating-point noise in the raw data (e.g. 15.0000000000002), but since the old code didn't round, I'll leave values unrounded to avoid silently altering the dataset, possibly only applying tolerance to the flag comparison itself.

Separately, I'm noting that results exactly equal to zero with a below-detection flag should map to NA with a "U" qualifier, and mapping out the full set of expected columns in the field data (sample IDs, analyte, result, units, flags, limits, and the various date/time fields).

Counting the columns confirms the 24-field structure for the FC dataset lines up, but I'm now second-guessing what `lab_sample` should represent -- whether it's a row label like a dish number, or the site label such as "RM 10_DUP," since `sample` might already serve that purpose.

I'll go with the design decision to reuse the label for both, even for duplicate rows, rather than digging into the contract reference further. For timing fields, DUP rows carry their own collect_time, and run_time/run_date get parsed using the config's clock-text parser, with validation requiring both to be present or the process stops; I also need to cross-check any date_of_analysis column against run_date when present, like the serialized date values in the summer dataset.

For dates stored as serial numbers or text, I'll write a helper that tries numeric parsing first (treating it as an Excel-style origin date) and falls back to flexible date parsing formats otherwise.

For the lab_qc table structure, I'm mapping out the full column set needed per facility, noting that result_text can be dropped since it's not required by the design even though one source system includes it. For method blank flagging, I'll set resultflag based on comparing results against the detection and reporting limits, marking values as censored or estimated accordingly, with repdl set to the reporting limit and results kept as originally reported.

I also need to filter out blank or trailing footer rows by checking for missing sample location values, and restrict to the expected sample types -- though I should watch for edge cases where trailing rows have stray notes that could break this filter, so I'll test it empirically. After confirming column types are correct, I'll write out the file and then source and compare it against expectations.


</thinking><span data-thinking-duration-ms="21439"></span></div>



<div><tool-call id="toolu_01B3aWdPUAJ3YfQYnPJNd3SC"><script type="application/json">{"version":2,"name":"write","input":{"file_path":"functions/ingest_tss.R","content":"# ingest_tss.R\n#\n# Reads one season of Soldotna Wastewater Treatment Plant (SWWTP) total s...[truncated, total 9128 chars]"},"caption":"functions/ingest_tss.R","captionLink":"functions/ingest_tss.R","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"# ingest_tss.R\n#\n# Reads one season of Soldotna Wastewater Treatment Plant (SWWTP) total suspended\n# solids (TSS) results and returns data that satisfy the `dat` contract\n# (reference/dat_contract.md), plus a lab QC table (method blanks).\n#\n# Usage:\n#   config \u003c- yaml::read_yaml(here::here(\"config.yml\"))\n#   tss \u003c- ingest_tss(\"spring\", config)\n#   tss$dat      # field results (contract columns)\n#   tss$lab_qc   # lab method blanks; never goes to the WQX export\n#\n# Year-specific settings come from config.yml and nothing about a particular year is\n# hardcoded here:\n#   sampling$\u003cseason>_date        collection date (the lab file's date columns are not used)\n#   files$tss$\u003cseason>            path, sheet, run_date, run_time (required: copy \"DATE OF\n#                                 ANALYSIS\" and \"TIME\" from the lab sheet header),\n#                                 rec_date, rec_time (optional: chain of custody)\n#   tss$lod, tss$loq              reporting limits, mg/L (QAPP Table 5)\n#\n# TSS is analyzed one to a few days after collection, so collect_date and run_date\n# differ and neither is derived from the other.\n\n# parse_clock() and parse_clock_text() are defined in ingest_fc.R\nif (!exists(\"parse_clock\", mode = \"function\")) {\n  source(here::here(\"functions\", \"ingest_fc.R\"), local = FALSE)\n}\n\ningest_tss \u003c- function(season,\n                       config,\n                       data_dir = here::here(\"data\"),\n                       sites_path = here::here(\"reference/baseline_sites.csv\")) {\n  stopifnot(season %in% c(\"spring\", \"summer\"))\n  suppressPackageStartupMessages({\n    library(dplyr); library(readxl); library(janitor)\n    library(stringr); library(lubridate); library(hms)\n  })\n\n  spec \u003c- config$files$tss[[season]]\n  if (is.null(spec)) stop(\"config.yml has no files$tss$\", season, call. = FALSE)\n  path \u003c- file.path(data_dir, spec$path)\n  if (!file.exists(path)) stop(\"TSS file not found: \", path, call. = FALSE)\n\n  collect_date \u003c- as.Date(config$sampling[[paste0(season, \"_date\")]])\n  if (is.na(collect_date)) stop(\"config.yml sampling$\", season, \"_date is missing\", call. = FALSE)\n\n  lod \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(config$tss$lod))\n  loq \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(config$tss$loq))\n  if (length(lod) != 1 || length(loq) != 1 || is.na(lod) || is.na(loq) || lod >= loq) {\n    stop(\"config.yml tss$lod and tss$loq must be set, with lod \u003c loq (mg/L).\", call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  run_date \u003c- if (is.null(spec$run_date)) as.Date(NA) else as.Date(spec$run_date)\n  run_time \u003c- if (is.null(spec$run_time)) as_hms(NA) else parse_clock_text(spec$run_time)\n  if (is.na(run_date) || is.na(run_time)) {\n    stop(\"config.yml files$tss$\", season, \" needs run_date and run_time. Copy 'DATE OF \",\n         \"ANALYSIS' and 'TIME' from the header of the lab sheet.\", call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  rec_date \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_date)) as.Date(NA) else as.Date(spec$rec_date)\n  rec_time \u003c- if (is.null(spec$rec_time)) as_hms(NA) else parse_clock_text(spec$rec_time)\n  if (is.na(rec_date) || is.na(rec_time)) {\n    warning(\"TSS \", season, \": receipt date/time not set in config.yml files$tss$\", season,\n            \" (rec_date, rec_time). Copy them from the chain of custody. \",\n            \"rec_date and rec_time are NA.\", call. = FALSE)\n    rec_date \u003c- as.Date(NA)\n    rec_time \u003c- as_hms(NA)\n  }\n\n  # Read everything as text; locate the table by its header row\n  raw \u003c- read_excel(path, sheet = spec$sheet, col_names = FALSE, col_types = \"text\",\n                    .name_repair = \"minimal\")\n  hdr_row \u003c- which(str_detect(raw[[1]], regex(\"^\\\\s*Sample_Location\\\\s*$\", ignore_case = TRUE)))[1]\n  if (is.na(hdr_row)) {\n    stop(\"No 'Sample_Location' header row found in \", basename(path),\n         \"; this file is not in the SWWTP TSS format.\", call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  tbl \u003c- raw[(hdr_row + 1):nrow(raw), , drop = FALSE]\n  names(tbl) \u003c- make_clean_names(unlist(raw[hdr_row, ], use.names = FALSE))\n  needed \u003c- c(\"sample_location\", \"sample_type\", \"sample_time\", \"ml\", \"dried_wt\", \"s_s_mg_l\")\n  if (!all(needed %in% names(tbl))) {\n    stop(\"TSS file \", basename(path), \" is missing expected columns: \",\n         paste(setdiff(needed, names(tbl)), collapse = \", \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\n    filter(!is.na(sample_location)) |>\n    mutate(sample_location = str_trim(sample_location),\n           sample_type     = str_to_upper(str_trim(sample_type)))\n\n  unknown \u003c- !tbl$sample_type %in% c(\"PS\", \"MB\")\n  if (any(unknown)) {\n    stop(\"Unexpected sample_type in \", basename(path), \" (expected PS or MB): \",\n         paste(unique(paste0(tbl$sample_location[unknown], \" [\", tbl$sample_type[unknown], \"]\")),\n               collapse = \"; \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  # Summer files carry Date_of_Analysis on every row; it must agree with config\n  if (\"date_of_analysis\" %in% names(tbl)) {\n    file_dates \u003c- parse_excel_date(tbl$date_of_analysis)\n    file_dates \u003c- unique(file_dates[!is.na(file_dates)])\n    if (length(file_dates) && any(file_dates != run_date)) {\n      stop(\"TSS file \", basename(path), \" has Date_of_Analysis \",\n           paste(file_dates, collapse = \", \"), \" but config.yml files$tss$\", season,\n           \"$run_date is \", run_date, call. = FALSE)\n    }\n  }\n\n  # Sites not sampled this event have no weights and no sample time\n  no_weight \u003c- is.na(tbl$dried_wt)\n  no_time   \u003c- is.na(tbl$sample_time)\n  not_sampled \u003c- tbl$sample_type == \"PS\" & no_weight & no_time\n  half \u003c- tbl$sample_type == \"PS\" & (no_weight != no_time)\n  if (any(half)) {\n    stop(\"TSS \", season, \": field rows with a weight but no sample time, or the reverse: \",\n         paste(tbl$sample_location[half], collapse = \"; \"), call. = FALSE)\n  }\n  if (any(not_sampled)) {\n    message(\"TSS \", season, \": dropped \", sum(not_sampled), \" site(s) with no weights and no \",\n            \"sample time (not sampled): \", paste(tbl$sample_location[not_sampled], collapse = \"; \"))\n  }\n  tbl \u003c- tbl[!not_sampled, ]\n\n  tbl \u003c- tbl |>\n    mutate(result = suppressWarnings(round(as.numeric(s_s_mg_l), 6)),\n           ml     = suppressWarnings(as.numeric(ml)))\n  bad \u003c- is.na(tbl$result)\n  if (any(bad)) {\n    stop(\"Non-numeric TSS results in \", basename(path), \": \",\n         paste(unique(paste0(tbl$sample_location[bad], \" = \", tbl$s_s_mg_l[bad])), collapse = \"; \"),\n         call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  is_mb \u003c- tbl$sample_type == \"MB\"\n  flag  \u003c- function(x) case_when(x \u003c lod ~ \"U\", x \u003c loq ~ \"J\", TRUE ~ \"=\")\n\n  lab_qc \u003c- tbl[is_mb, ] |>\n    transmute(\n      sample_type = \"MB\",\n      qc_group    = \"blank\",\n      analyte     = \"Total suspended solids\",\n      analytical_method = \"2540-D\",\n      result,\n      resultflag  = flag(result),\n      repdl       = loq,\n      units       = \"mg/L\",\n      amount_spiked = NA_real_, initial_conc = NA_real_, percent_recovered = NA_real_,\n      rec_limit_low = NA_real_, rec_limit_high = NA_real_, sample_rpd = NA_real_,\n      rpd_limit_low = NA_real_, rpd_limit_high = NA_real_,\n      anl_batch = NA_character_, prp_batch = NA_character_, sdg = NA_character_,\n      lab_sample_id = sample_location,\n      sample_id     = sample_location,\n      collect_date  = collect_date,\n      run_date      = run_date,\n      dissolved     = NA_character_,\n      lab_name      = \"SWWTP\",\n      season        = season\n    )\n\n  field \u003c- tbl[!is_mb, ]\n  sites \u003c- resolve_rm_site(field$sample_location, sites_path)\n  if (anyNA(sites$monitoring_location_id)) {\n    stop(\"TSS field labels that are lab QC rows but marked PS: \",\n         paste(field$sample_location[is.na(sites$monitoring_location_id)], collapse = \"; \"),\n         call. = FALSE)\n  }\n\n  dat \u003c- field |>\n    mutate(\n      monitoring_location_id = sites$monitoring_location_id,\n      sample_condition       = sites$sample_condition,\n      resultflag             = flag(result),\n      # A value below the LOD is a non-detect: no numeric result, flagged \"U\"\n      result                 = if_else(result \u003c lod, NA_real_, result)\n    ) |>\n    transmute(\n      sample       = sample_location,\n      lab_sample   = sample_location,\n      lab_name     = \"SWWTP\",\n      analyte      = \"Total suspended solids\",\n      result,\n      units        = \"mg/L\",\n      resultflag,\n      loq          = loq,\n      lod          = lod,\n      collect_date = collect_date,\n      collect_time = parse_clock(sample_time),\n      rec_date     = rec_date,\n      rec_time     = rec_time,\n      run_date     = run_date,\n      run_time     = run_time,\n      analytical_method = \"2540-D\",\n      epa_analysis_id   = \"2540-D\",\n      context_code = \"APHA\",\n      monitoring_location_id,\n      note         = paste0(\"Sample volume = \", ml, \" mL\"),\n      sample_type  = \"PS\",\n      sample_condition,\n      dissolved    = NA_character_,\n      result_sample_fraction = NA_character_\n    )\n\n  list(dat = dat, lab_qc = lab_qc)\n}\n\n# Excel serial number (text) or an ISO / M-D-Y date string\nparse_excel_date \u003c- function(x) {\n  v \u003c- suppressWarnings(as.numeric(x))\n  out \u003c- as.Date(v, origin = \"1899-12-30\")\n  txt \u003c- is.na(v) & !is.na(x)\n  if (any(txt)) {\n    out[txt] \u003c- as.Date(lubridate::parse_date_time(x[txt], orders = c(\"Ymd\", \"mdY\", \"mdy\"),\n                                                   quiet = TRUE))\n  }\n  out\n}\n","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

<div><tool-call id="toolu_016dLa9LxzcHcK8rzqQNaP4V"><script type="application/json">{"version":2,"name":"executeCode","input":{"code":"library(dplyr)\nconfig \u003c- yaml::read_yaml(here::here(\"config.yml\"))\nsource(here::here(\"func...[truncated, total 1428 chars]","description":"Source ingest_tss and compare with old dat_tss"},"description":"Source ingest_tss and compare with old dat_tss","inputPreview":{"type":"code","value":"library(dplyr)\nconfig \u003c- yaml::read_yaml(here::here(\"config.yml\"))\nsource(here::here(\"functions/ingest_fc.R\")); source(here::here(\"functions/resolve_rm_site.R\")); source(here::here(\"functions/ingest_tss.R\"))\nsp_t \u003c- ingest_tss(\"spring\", config); su_t \u003c- ingest_tss(\"summer\", config)\nnew_tss \u003c- bind_rows(sp_t$dat, su_t$dat)\nc(spring = nrow(sp_t$dat), summer = nrow(su_t$dat), lab_spring = nrow(sp_t$lab_qc), lab_summer = nrow(su_t$lab_qc), cols = ncol(new_tss))\ncmp \u003c- full_join(\n  dat_tss |> transmute(monitoring_location_id = as.character(monitoring_location_id), collect_date, sample_condition = coalesce(sample_condition, \"-\"), old_result = result, old_flag = resultflag, old_time = as.character(collect_time)),\n  new_tss |> transmute(monitoring_location_id, collect_date, sample_condition = coalesce(sample_condition, \"-\"), new_result = result, new_flag = resultflag, new_time = as.character(collect_time), sample),\n  by = c(\"monitoring_location_id\", \"collect_date\", \"sample_condition\"))\nc(unmatched = sum(is.na(cmp$old_result) & is.na(cmp$new_result) & (is.na(cmp$old_flag) | is.na(cmp$new_flag))))\ncmp |> filter(is.na(old_flag) | is.na(new_flag) | old_flag != new_flag | abs(coalesce(old_result,-1) - coalesce(new_result,-1)) > 1e-6 | old_time != new_time) |> as.data.frame()\nnames(new_tss)\nsp_t$lab_qc |> select(sample_id, result, resultflag, collect_date, run_date) \nsu_t$lab_qc |> select(sample_id, result, resultflag)","language":"r"}}</script></tool-call></div>

`functions/ingest_tss.R` is written and checked against the old `dat_tss`. It returns `list(dat, lab_qc)` for one season, and the contract columns come out as designed.

| | Spring | Summer |
|---|---|---|
| `dat` rows | 22 | 24 |
| `lab_qc` rows | 4 | 4 |

**Comparison with the old `dat_tss`.** The old table had 48 rows and the new `dat` has 46. All 46 new rows match on site, date and condition, and results, flags and collect times agree. The two rows only in the old table are spring RM 6.5 and RM 10.1, the sites that weren't visited. The old chunk had kept them as non-detects. The function now drops them with a message. No flags changed with the LOD moving from 0.5 to 0.31, because no 2025 field value falls between 0.31 and 0.5.

**Lab blanks.** The spring blanks read 0.333, 0, 0.333 and 0 mg/L, so the two 0.333 values are flagged `J` (between the 0.31 LOD and the 1.0 LOQ), and the two zeros are `U`. All four summer blanks are 0 (`U`). Their `collect_date` is the config date and `run_date` is the config run date.

**Behaviors to know:**
- **Receipt:** It warns and sets receipt to NA for both seasons, because `rec_date` and `rec_time` are blank in the config.
- **Results rounded:** Results are rounded to 6 decimals, only to remove spreadsheet noise like `15.0000000000002`. It does not change any reported value.
- **Helpers:** `ingest_tss.R` borrows `parse_clock()` and `parse_clock_text()` from `ingest_fc.R`, and sources that file if they aren't loaded. Moving them into a shared file is a small cleanup I'd do before `ingest_ysi.R`, since YSI needs them too.
- **Summer sheet:** The cross-check of `Date_of_Analysis` against the config run date passed.
- **Earlier estimate was off:** I said summer would have 22 field rows or fewer. It has 24, which matches the old table.
